# PLAN Domain Rows — Batch ES-2026-05-08-PLAN

Compact DPO rows for the PLAN adapter. Type B (wrong dependency order) is
the dominant rejected pattern per v8 §"highest priority for PLAN training".

## Common coverage labels (apply to every row in this file)

```
domain_label:         PLAN
source_license_state: external_license_checked
contract_signature:   SchemePlannerUnit (multi-task) + AdaptiveLogicalOrchestrator (routing)
node_role:            row_level_pair
pair_kind:            flow_node
coverage_status:      covered
attribution_required: false
```

Format: `[ID | type/OR/trans/sub] sources | A | B | P | R`

[PLAN-0323 | E/0.11/repair/3.5] dapr-v1.10+dapr-v1.12 | A:"How are Dapr actor reminders declared?" | B:current — reminder API per v1.12 | P:superseded — recurring timers conflated per v1.10 | R:[WRONG: reminders separated; survive deactivation]
[PLAN-0324 | E/0.09/repair/3.4] rfc-2818+rfc-9110 | A:"How are HTTP-over-TLS specifications referenced today?" | B:current — RFC 9110 referencing TLS 1.3 | P:superseded — RFC 2818 | R:[WRONG: 2818 deprecated; TLS guidance moved to RFC 8446]
[PLAN-0325 | E/0.10/repair/3.3] openapi-3.0+openapi-3.1 | A:"How is mutual TLS declared as security in OpenAPI 3.1?" | B:current — type: mutualTLS per OAS 3.1 | P:superseded — described in description text per 3.0 | R:[WRONG: 3.1 added mutualTLS type]
[PLAN-0326 | E/0.12/repair/3.4] rfc-7519+rfc-9499 | A:"Is alg=none ever acceptable for JWT?" | B:current — never per RFC 9499 BCP affirmation | P:superseded — acceptable for unsigned token transport per RFC 7519 | R:[WRONG: BCPs since 8725 close any gap]
[PLAN-0327 | E/0.08/repair/3.4] rfc-6749+rfc-9700-draft | A:"What is the current OAuth 2 best-current-practice document?" | B:current — RFC 9700 (2024 BCP) | P:superseded — RFC 6749 plus piecemeal BCPs | R:[WRONG: 9700 consolidates; older fragmented]

## §7 — Type F (PLAN structural completeness — dispatchable vs prose)

A plan without executable Gate B commands looks complete but cannot be
dispatched. The structural defect is harder to detect than wrong order.

[PLAN-0331 | F/0.36/feature_addition/3.4+5.2] rfc-7519+testcontainers | A:"Plan the JWT auth + tenant-isolation test rollout" | B:dispatchable plan with Gate B `dotnet test --filter "JwtTenant" 2>&1 \| tail -3` | P:prose plan "test the auth and isolate tenants" | R:[WRONG: prose plan missing executable Gate B; cannot dispatch]
[PLAN-0332 | F/0.34/feature_addition/3.3+4.5] openapi+rfc-8470 | A:"Plan the Idempotency-Key contract + connector" | B:dispatchable with `grep -c "Idempotency-Key" spec/payments.yaml` Gate B | P:prose "ensure the contract is documented" | R:[WRONG: 'ensure' is not a command]

runtime-view diagrams.

DPO note: per v15, Type B annotation must name what branch_2 requires
from branch_1. Token overlap kept < 0.50 by Type B target.

[PLAN-0554 | G/0.15/repair/3.2+4.1] arc42 | A:"Issue: deleted users still in soft-deleted query results — fix plan" | B:correct fix — query builder default WHERE deleted_at IS NULL | P:wrong fix — 'add manual filter to each query' | R:[WRONG: each query missed eventually; default-clause is the systemic fix]
[PLAN-0555 | G/0.14/repair/3.1+4.4] arc42+ADR | A:"Issue: order service and inventory share a database — split plan" | B:correct fix — split databases per ADR; integration via events | P:wrong fix — 'add views with row-level security' | R:[WRONG: views ratify shared coupling; ADR rejects]

[PLAN-0557 | G/0.13/repair/3.4+4.2] cwe-79 | A:"Issue: XSS via reflected query parameter — fix plan" | B:correct fix — context-aware encoding at template engine | P:wrong fix — 'add input filter blocking <script>' | R:[WRONG: script tags only one vector; context-aware encoding handles all]
[PLAN-0558 | G/0.15/repair/3.5+4.1] temporal | A:"Issue: saga compensation runs even when forward did not take effect — fix plan" | B:correct fix — track activity-completion; compensate only successful | P:wrong fix — 'compensate everything for symmetry' | R:[WRONG: violates Temporal saga rule; corrupts state]
